leader_trait_libra_kotori_fan = {
	leader_trait_type = negative
	inline_script = {
		script = trait/icon_negative
		ICON = "GFX_leader_trait_libra_kotori_fan"
		COUNCIL = yes
		TIER = none
	}
	councilor_modifier = {
		country_councilor_influence_produces_add = 2
		tradition_cost_empire_size_mult = 0.15
		planetary_ascension_effect_mult = -0.25
	}
	leader_class = { commander official scientist }
	leader_potential_add = {
		is_ruler = yes
	}
	selectable_weight = {
		weight = 0.3
	}
}

# #############
# Corrupt Leader Traits
#############

leader_trait_libra_corrupt_beacon = {
	leader_trait_type = veteran
	force_councilor_trait = yes
	inline_script = {
		script = trait/libra_trait
		TYPE = corrupt
		CLASS = leader
		ICON = GFX_leader_trait_libra_corrupt_beacon
		RARITY = veteran
		COUNCIL = no
		TIER = none
	}
	triggered_self_modifier = {
		potential = { has_base_skill < 10 }
		species_leader_exp_gain = 0.05
		mult = value:libra_corrupt_trait_effect_mult
	}
	triggered_self_modifier = {
		potential = { has_base_skill >= 10 }
		leaders_upkeep_mult = -0.05
		mult = value:libra_corrupt_trait_effect_mult
	}
	custom_tooltip_with_modifiers = leader_trait_libra_corrupt_beacon_tt
	leader_class = { commander official scientist }
	on_gained_effect = {
		set_leader_flag = libra_has_corrupt_leader_trait
		leader_event = { id = libra_fairy.1400 days = 360 random = 720 }
	}
	leader_potential_add = {
		is_hidden = no
		not = { has_leader_flag = libra_has_corrupt_leader_trait }
		or = {
			has_base_skill = 9
			has_base_skill = 10
			num_leader_traits = {
				value > 0
				negative = yes
			}
		}
		exists = owner
		owner = { not = { has_tradition = tr_libra_degeneracy_2 } }
	}
	selectable_weight = {
		weight = @subclass_trait_weight
		modifier = {
			factor = 3
			num_leader_traits = {
				value > 1
				negative = yes
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 3
			num_leader_traits = {
				value > 2
				count_tiers = yes
				negative = yes
			}
		}
	}
	ai_weight = {
		factor = 2
		modifier = {
			factor = 4
			num_leader_traits = {
				value > 1
				negative = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

leader_trait_libra_corrupt_generic_a = {
	leader_trait_type = veteran
	inline_script = {
		script = traits/libra_corrupt_veteran_trait
		CLASS = leader
		ICON = GFX_leader_trait_libra_corrupt_generic_a
		COUNCIL = no
	}
	self_modifier = {
		species_leader_exp_gain = 0.15
	}
	triggered_self_modifier = {
		potential = { is_councilor = no }
		leader_skill_add = 1
		mult = value:libra_corrupt_trait_effect_mult
	}
	leader_class = { commander official scientist }
	leader_potential_add = {
		can_select_libra_corrupt_leader_traits = yes
		has_council_subclass = no
		has_governor_subclass = no
	}
}

		leader_officials_society_research_produces_add = 9

leader_trait_libra_corrupt_node_a = {
	leader_trait_type = veteran
	inline_script = {
		script = traits/libra_corrupt_veteran_trait
		CLASS = leader
		ICON = GFX_leader_trait_libra_corrupt_generic_f
		COUNCIL = yes
	}
	triggered_councilor_modifier = {
		council_agenda_progress_speed = 0.05
		mult = value:libra_corrupt_trait_effect_mult
	}
	leader_class = { commander official scientist }
	leader_potential_add = {
		exists = owner
		owner = {
			is_gestalt = yes
			has_tradition = tr_libra_degeneracy_2
		}
		has_base_skill > 3
		has_base_skill <= 10
		not = { has_leader_flag = libra_has_corrupt_leader_trait }
		or = {
			is_councilor_type = councilor_gestalt_growth
			is_councilor_type = councilor_gestalt_cognitive
			is_councilor_type = councilor_gestalt_legion
			is_councilor_type = councilor_gestalt_regulatory
		}
	}
}

		ICON = "GFX_leader_trait_libra_scientist_governor_corrupt_destiny_a"

		COUNCIL = no
	}
	on_gained_effect = {
		add_trait = {
			trait = random_negative
			show_message = yes
		}
		owner = {
			country_event = { id = timeline.50 } # Timeline First Leader with Destiny Trait
		}
	}
	triggered_planet_modifier = {
		custom_tooltip = leader_trait_libra_scientist_governor_corrupt_destiny_a_tt_1
		planet_jobs_productive_produces_mult = 0.3
		planet_buildings_physics_research_produces_mult = 0.4
		planet_buildings_society_research_produces_mult = 0.4
		planet_buildings_engineering_research_produces_mult = 0.4
	}
	triggered_planet_modifier = {
		potential = { exists = planet }
		custom_tooltip = BLANK_STRING
		planet_jobs_productive_produces_mult = -0.06
		mult = planet.trigger:num_modifiers
	}
	triggered_sector_modifier = {
		custom_tooltip = leader_trait_libra_scientist_governor_corrupt_destiny_a_tt_2
		planet_jobs_productive_produces_mult = 0.15
		planet_buildings_physics_research_produces_mult = 0.2
		planet_buildings_society_research_produces_mult = 0.2
		planet_buildings_engineering_research_produces_mult = 0.2
	}
	triggered_sector_modifier = {
		potential = { exists = planet }
		custom_tooltip = BLANK_STRING
		planet_jobs_productive_produces_mult = -0.03
		mult = planet.trigger:num_modifiers
	}
	leader_class = { scientist }
	leader_potential_add = {
		has_paragon_dlc = yes
		has_trait = subclass_scientist_governor
	}
}

		script = traits/libra_psionic_veteran_trait_tier1
		CLASS = commander
		ICON = GFX_leader_trait_agrarian_upbringing
		COUNCIL = no
	}
	planet_modifier = {
		planet_farmers_food_produces_add = 1
		planet_libra_harmonizers_food_produces_add = 1
	}
	sector_modifier = {
		planet_farmers_food_produces_add = 0.5
		planet_libra_harmonizers_food_produces_add = 0.5
	}
	leader_class = { commander }
	leader_potential_add = {
		can_use_libra_psionic_veteran_traits = yes
		has_trait = subclass_commander_governor
		exists = owner
		owner = { country_uses_food = yes }
	}
	inline_script = traits/libra_psionic_veteran_trait_weights
	ai_weight = {
		factor = @libra_psionic_veteran_trait_extra_weight
		modifier = {
			factor = @libra_designation_bonus
			exists = planet
			planet = { libra_has_farming_designation = yes }
		}
	}
}

leader_trait_libra_psionic_investor = {
	inline_script = {
		script = traits/libra_psionic_common_trait_tier1
		CLASS = official
		ICON = GFX_leader_trait_investor
		COUNCIL = no
	}
	planet_modifier = {
		planet_jobs_trade_produces_mult = 0.16
	}
	sector_modifier = {
		planet_jobs_trade_produces_mult = 0.08
	}
	leader_class = { official }
	#initial = no
	leader_potential_add = {
		can_use_libra_psionic_leader_traits = yes
	}
	on_gained_effect = {
		set_leader_flag = has_libra_extra_common_trait
	}
	selectable_weight = {
		weight = @class_trait_weight
		inline_script = paragon/governor_weight_mult
		inline_script = traits/libra_psionic_common_trait_weight
	}
	ai_weight = {
		factor = @libra_psionic_common_trait_ai_weight
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			owner = {
				libra_ai_preferences_trade = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

	inline_script = {
		script = traits/libra_psionic_common_trait_tier1
		CLASS = official
		ICON = GFX_leader_trait_settler
		COUNCIL = no
	}
	triggered_planet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = { is_gestalt = no }
		}
		pop_happiness = 0.1
	}
	triggered_sector_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = { is_gestalt = no }
		}
		pop_happiness = 0.05
	}
	triggered_planet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = { is_gestalt = yes }
		}
		planet_stability_add = 10
	}
	triggered_sector_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner

leader_trait_libra_psionic_governor_physics_focus = {
	inline_script = {
		script = traits/libra_psionic_veteran_trait_tier1
		CLASS = scientist
		ICON = GFX_leader_trait_physics_focus
		COUNCIL = no
	}
	triggered_planet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = { is_gestalt = no }
		}
		planet_physicists_physics_research_produces_add = 0.5
		planet_physicists_consumer_goods_upkeep_add = 0.25
		planet_libra_harmonizers_physics_research_produces_add = 0.5
		planet_libra_harmonizers_consumer_goods_upkeep_add = 0.25
	}
	triggered_planet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = { is_gestalt = yes }
		}
		planet_physicists_physics_research_produces_add = 0.5
		planet_physicists_energy_upkeep_add = 0.5
		planet_libra_harmonizers_physics_research_produces_add = 0.5
		planet_libra_harmonizers_minerals_upkeep_add = 0.5
	}
	triggered_sector_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = { is_gestalt = no }
		}
		planet_physicists_physics_research_produces_add = 0.25
		planet_physicists_consumer_goods_upkeep_add = 0.125
		planet_libra_harmonizers_physics_research_produces_add = 0.25
		planet_libra_harmonizers_consumer_goods_upkeep_add = 0125
	}
	triggered_sector_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = { is_gestalt = yes }
		}
		planet_physicists_physics_research_produces_add = 0.25
		planet_physicists_energy_upkeep_add = 0.25
		planet_libra_harmonizers_physics_research_produces_add = 0.25
		planet_libra_harmonizers_minerals_upkeep_add = 0.25
	}
	leader_class = { scientist }
	leader_potential_add = {
		can_use_libra_psionic_veteran_traits = yes
		has_trait = subclass_scientist_governor
	}
	inline_script = traits/libra_psionic_veteran_trait_weights

	leader_trait_type = veteran
	inline_script = {
		script = trait/libra_trait
		TYPE = machine
		RARITY = veteran
		CLASS = official
		ICON = GFX_leader_trait_libra_synthetic_official_governor_b
		COUNCIL = no
		TIER = 1
	}
	planet_modifier = {
		pop_artisan_bonus_workforce_mult = 0.3
	}
	sector_modifier = {
		pop_artisan_bonus_workforce_mult = 0.15
	}
	leader_class = { official }
	leader_potential_add = {
		can_use_libra_synthetic_leader_traits = yes
		has_trait = subclass_official_governor
	}
	prerequisites = { tech_synthetic_leaders }
	inline_script = traits/libra_synthetic_veteran_trait_weights
	ai_weight = {
		factor = @libra_synthetic_veteran_trait_extra_weight
	}
}

leader_trait_libra_synthetic_official_delegate_a = {
	leader_trait_type = veteran
	inline_script = {
		script = trait/libra_trait
		TYPE = machine
		RARITY = veteran
		CLASS = official
		ICON = GFX_leader_trait_libra_synthetic_official_delegate_a
		COUNCIL = no
		TIER = 1
	}
	federation_modifier = {
		cohesion_add = 1
		federation_experience_add = 2
	}
	leader_class = { official }
	leader_potential_add = {
		can_use_libra_synthetic_leader_traits = yes
		has_trait = subclass_official_delegate
	}
	prerequisites = { tech_synthetic_leaders }
	inline_script = traits/libra_synthetic_veteran_trait_weights
	ai_weight = {
		factor = @libra_synthetic_veteran_trait_extra_weight
		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			owner = { has_federation = no }
		}
	}
}

leader_trait_libra_synthetic_official_delegate_c = {
	leader_trait_type = veteran
	inline_script = {
		script = trait/libra_trait
		TYPE = machine
		RARITY = veteran
		CLASS = official
		ICON = GFX_leader_trait_libra_synthetic_official_delegate_c
		COUNCIL = no
		TIER = 1
	}
	galcom_modifier = {
		diplo_weight_supporting_mult = 0.1
	}
	custom_tooltip_with_modifiers = leader_trait_libra_synthetic_official_delegate_c_1_tt
	leader_class = { official }
	leader_potential_add = {
		can_use_libra_synthetic_leader_traits = yes
		has_trait = subclass_official_delegate
	}
	prerequisites = { tech_synthetic_leaders }
	on_gained_effect = {
		set_leader_flag = has_libra_extra_veteran_trait
		set_leader_flag = libra_enable_resolution_past_effect
	}
	selectable_weight = {
		weight = @subclass_trait_weight
		inline_script = traits/libra_synthetic_veteran_trait_weight
	}
	ai_weight = {
		factor = @libra_synthetic_veteran_trait_extra_weight
	}
}

			exists = owner

		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = { is_pleasure_seeker_empire = yes }
		}
		planet_pop_assembly_mult = 0.1
	}
	leader_class = { official }
	leader_potential_add = {
		can_use_libra_synthetic_leader_traits = yes
		has_trait = subclass_official_economy_councilor

	prerequisites = { tech_synthetic_leaders }
	inline_script = traits/libra_synthetic_veteran_trait_weights

		factor = @libra_synthetic_veteran_trait_extra_weight

leader_trait_libra_commander_admiral_destiny_a = {
	inline_script = {
		script = traits/libra_common_destiny_trait
		ICON = "GFX_leader_trait_libra_commander_admiral_destiny_a"
		CLASS = commander
		COUNCIL = no
	}
	on_gained_effect = {
		owner = {
			country_event = { id = timeline.50 } # Timeline First Leader with Destiny Trait
		}
	}
	fleet_modifier = {
		ship_cloaking_strength_add = 5
		ship_shield_penetration_add = 0.4
		ship_armor_penetration_add = 0.4
	}
	triggered_fleet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = from
			from = { is_cloaked = yes }
		}
		custom_tooltip = leader_trait_libra_commander_admiral_destiny_a_tt
		not_potential_override_text_key = BLANK_STRING
		show_if_not_potential = yes
		ship_speed_mult = 0.5
		ship_speed_reduction = -0.4
		ship_jumpdrive_cooldown_mult = -0.4
	}
	leader_class = { commander }
	prerequisites = { tech_cloaking_2 }
	leader_potential_add = {
		has_paragon_dlc = yes
		has_trait = subclass_commander_admiral
	}
	background_icon = "GFX_leader_background_libra_destiny_3"
}

leader_trait_libra_ceremony = {
	force_councilor_trait = yes
	inline_script = {
		script = trait/libra_trait
		TYPE = default
		CLASS = leader
		ICON = "GFX_leader_trait_libra_ceremony"
		RARITY = paragon
		COUNCIL = triggered
		TIER = none
	}

	self_modifier = {
		leader_trait_selection_options_add = 1
	}

	triggered_self_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = no
			leader_class = commander
		}
		leader_commanders_physics_research_produces_add = 9
		leader_commanders_alloys_produces_add = 2
		mult = value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_traits_mult
	}
	triggered_self_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = no
			leader_class = scientist
		}
		leader_scientists_society_research_produces_add = 9
		leader_scientists_energy_produces_add = 8
		mult = value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_traits_mult
	}
	triggered_self_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = no
			leader_class = official
		}
		leader_officials_engineering_research_produces_add = 9
		leader_officials_minerals_produces_add = 6
		mult = value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_traits_mult
	}

	triggered_councilor_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = yes
		}
		planet_jobs_produces_mult = 0.01
		planet_buildings_produces_mult = 0.01
		mult = value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_traits_mult
	}

	custom_tooltip_with_modifiers = leader_trait_libra_traits_effect_tt
	leader_class = { scientist official commander }
	initial = no
	randomized = no
}

	force_councilor_trait = yes

		TIER = none
	}

		ship_weapon_damage = 0.01
		ship_fire_rate_mult = 0.01
		mult = value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_service_mult
	}
	triggered_army_modifier = {
		army_damage_mult = 0.01
		army_health = 0.01
		mult = value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_service_mult
	}
	triggered_planet_modifier = {
		planet_deposits_produces_mult = 0.01
		planet_technician_produces_mult = 0.01
		planet_miners_produces_mult = 0.01
		planet_farmers_produces_mult = 0.01
		mult = value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_service_mult
	}
	triggered_planet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = { has_tradition = tr_libra_grandmastery_4 }
		}
		planet_building_capacity_add = 1
	}
	triggered_sector_modifier = {
		planet_deposits_produces_mult = 0.005
		planet_technician_produces_mult = 0.005
		planet_miners_produces_mult = 0.005
		planet_farmers_produces_mult = 0.005
		mult = value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_service_mult
	}
	triggered_councilor_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = yes
		}
		councilor_skill_add = 1
	}
	triggered_background_planet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = yes
			exists = FROM
			FROM = {
				exists = owner
				owner = {
					is_same_value = root.owner
					has_tradition = tr_libra_grandmastery_4
				}
			}
		}
		job_soldier_or_warrior_add = 100
		mult = value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_service_mult
	}
	custom_tooltip_with_modifiers = leader_trait_libra_service_effect_tt
	leader_class = { scientist official commander }
	initial = no
	randomized = no
	#opposites = { leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_commander_max leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_official_max leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist leader_trait_libra_grandmastery_scientist_max }
